Before any next native trial, reuse established supervised/sequence models and BAGI's existing public-effect collector to preregister ONE matched, source-disjoint **history-conditioned outcome/aliasing** comparison against action-only and shuffled-history controls, with independently learned Workshop behavior and frozen XLand behavior, matched real-step accounting and separate per-domain gates. If independently learned Workshop behavior or adequate diverse TRAIN coverage cannot be verified, perform TRAIN-only coverage/provenance work instead of claiming transfer. This append-only result audit made **zero new native simulator actions, optimizer updates, teacher queries, secrets/deployment/main changes**, and introduced no duplicate workflow or automation.

### G2-E5-P0 read-only TRAIN-history predictive-support audit — NO MEMORY PROMOTION (2026-10-26)

- **Source and scope:** reuse existing G2-E1 native artifact `12588790887` (ZIP SHA256 `aaf8eb011763bf47e1d196771e257eb13c4eb35294bbeed2b71757ade525f073`) and G2-E2-P0 Workshop DQN artifact `12596191672` (ZIP SHA256 `b000a02a2eacfb8aa07befa151e0bbfb2c5bf7fd430f8bd896bd11f90f57436a`), both independently redownloaded/digest-verified. Read **only E1 `*_train_*`** and E2 **`actor_index==0` TRAIN** arrays with `numpy.load(allow_pickle=False)`; no E1/E2 heldout outcomes were read, scored, or used for selection. Methods, exact numbers and limitations: `docs/GENERAL_BRAIN_G2_E5_P0_TRAIN_ONLY_HISTORY_CROSSFIT.md`, pure-Numpy `bagi/experiments/g2_e5_train_history_audit.py`, seven simulator-free tests.
- **Method:** leave-one-TRAIN-layout-out XLand (15 layouts), leave-one-TRAIN-episode-out Workshop (one geometry only), exact public-view + executed-action change-rate table versus Laplace-smoothed action-frequency fallback; compare true previous 1/2/4 executed actions with fixed within-episode shuffled-action-history control. E1 XLand original 115 public features already include last-four executed actions; compare separately to 87-feature no-history view. This is TRAIN crossfit, not external validation or a learned neural policy.
- **XLand 3,244 TRAIN actions/180 episodes:** action-only Brier **0.056489** versus original 115-public-view+action **0.057975**, unchanged by appending true 4 actions; exact-key cross-layout support only **193/3244**. Removing encoded history and adding back 2 past actions gives **0.056093** (tiny exploratory 0.000396 improvement over action-only, only 421/3244 exact-key matches); 4 past actions returns to **0.057975**. Frozen BC-only slice also does not beat action-only (0.065804 vs 0.063546). This does NOT establish memory value, learned action outcomes or physical manipulation.
- **Workshop E2 TRAIN, 2,048 actions/52 episodes per DQN seed, zero native TRAIN goal rewards:** current-view+action Brier **0.011524/0.007840** seeds17/29 versus action-only **0.200828/0.202626**, but appending 4 true actions **worsens** Brier to **0.147998/0.158625** as exact-key support drops from **1986/2017** to **682/665** rows out of 2048. Workshop E1 fixed script is duplicated; the random-only slice also worsens from view+action **0.051325** to 4-action **0.177289**. One fixed Workshop geometry is not layout transfer. Shuffled history can eliminate collisions merely by fragmenting keys.
- **Decision:** **NO MEMORY PROMOTION**; neither repeated easy public transitions nor adding action history establishes useful belief or task-independent competence. Existing G2-E1/E2/E3/E4 FAIL/STOP results unchanged. Next require truly rewarded independently learned Workshop policy and fresh source-disjoint coverage before a prospective matched reactive/history/shuffled-history effect-prediction test, with action-only baseline and separate XLand physical success evaluator. Reuse established PPO/RecurrentPPO and public-effect code, not another BLUE-specific heuristic. **0 new native actions, 0 policy updates, 0 teacher queries, 0 TEST evaluations, 0 new workflow/automation, 0 secrets/deployments/main changes** this bounded iteration.

Raw/scaled and two model seeds are correlated same-task conditions.
